##################################################
# COTC - Family names
#
# Dynasties can randomly be named after the location, which ends up as
# "New Liberty II-b". So we overwrite this generated name.
#
# The random_list should only select from the valid ones, so Terran
# randomly selects from all its name_lists
##################################################

cotc_assign_family_name_effect = {
	random_list = {
		10 = {
			trigger = { $CULTURE$ = { has_name_list = name_list_cotc_american } }
			cotc_family_name_cotc_american_effect = yes
		}
		10 = {
			trigger = { $CULTURE$ = { has_name_list = name_list_cotc_angolan } }
			cotc_family_name_cotc_angolan_effect = yes

# Gives a character a family name that is not a place name.
#
# A lowborn has no dynasty, so one is generated from a titleless dummy of the same
# culture. Anyone else may already carry a dynasty the engine named after a title, 
# so they are caught by dynasty check: not canon (defined in common/dynasties), and not already renamed.
cotc_fix_family_name_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_lowborn = yes }
		generate_family_name_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			dynasty ?= {
				NOT = { cotc_is_canon_dynasty_trigger = yes }
				NOT = { has_variable = cotc_family_name_set }
			}
		}
		cotc_assign_family_name_effect = { CULTURE = culture }
	}
}
